9.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal
Nonce reuse across sessions Full key share revealed if same (d_i, e_i) used twice with different messages CSPRNG not re-seeded or state persisted Mark nonces consumed after use; Nonce::take() pattern with Option<Nonce>
Non-deterministic commitment list ordering Different rho_i per party → different R → protocol abort Sort not applied before hashing RFC 9591: sort by signer_id ascending; test fixture checks determinism
Missing domain separation in hash Cross-protocol hash collisions; binding factor forgeable No "FROST-{ciphersuite}-v1" prefix Audit transcript.rs; compare byte-for-byte with RFC 9591
E_i = identity (zero nonce) Binding nonce contributes nothing; reduces attack surface to hiding nonce only Accepting e_i = 0 Reject NonceCommitment where hiding or binding is identity point
rho_i computed without per-signer i prefix All signers get identical binding factor Using common hash prefix RFC 9591 sec. 4.3: each rho_i includes encoded i; fixture verifies distinct values
Partial commitment_list (only t signers, not all n), or Nonce serialized to disk R computed over wrong set / secret nonce exposed Aggregator uses filtered list inconsistently; mistaken persistence for "resume" Binding factors and group nonce must use identical list by reference; Nonce must not implement Serialize, ZeroizeOnDrop only

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • round1_commit: 2 scalar muls (g^d_i, g^e_i); ~0.1–0.3 ms per signer. compute_group_nonce: n scalar muls + n point additions; parallelizable with Rayon over the n contributions.
  • compute_binding_factors for n signers: one hash per signer, each input O(n) bytes (full commitment list) → total O(n²) bytes hashed; at n=100 ≈ 980 KB hashed. Use streaming hash (sha2::Sha512 update API) to avoid large buffer allocation — this dominates the round-1 cost for large n.
  • Memory: NonceCommitment = 2 compressed Points + ShareId = 66+4 = 70 bytes; 100 signers = 7 KB commitment list, fits in L1.

9.8 Edge Cases

Case Expected behavior
Single signer (t = n = 1) R = D_1 + rho_1 * E_1; binding factor non-trivial even for 1 signer
msg is empty [], or 1 MB Valid — binding factors computed over empty message (RFC 9591 allows); streaming hash handles 1 MB with no buffer allocation limit
commitment_list with identity point D_i or E_i Err(IdentityCommitment)
commitment_list sorted already, or in reverse order No re-sort needed (idempotent); sort applied, result identical to sorted case
Repeated round1_commit call without consuming first nonce Previous Nonce dropped/zeroized before second call

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Nonce reuse (same (d_i, e_i) for two sign sessions) Attacker observes two partial signatures; recovers share_i Single party's key share exposed Stateful nonce consumption; Option<Nonce> set None after use Rotate key share via proactive refresh (Phase 12); disqualify party
Binding factor mismatch (different R values) Phase 10 aggregation fails signature verification Sign session aborts; no invalid signature produced Detect at aggregator: all z_i must be consistent with agreed R Re-run Round 1 with fresh nonces; investigate message delivery
CSPRNG weak entropy Nonces guessable; security reduced All sessions from affected RNG Use OS getrandom; no user-seeded RNG Audit RNG source; rotate nonces; re-deal if key shares affected
Aggregator omits a signer's commitment That signer's rho_i is undefined; partial sig invalid One signer excluded from session Signer detects missing commitment via consistency check Re-submit commitment; run new Round 1

9.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: tss_schnorr_round1_commit_duration_us histogram per signer call; tss_schnorr_binding_factor_compute_duration_ms{n_signers} histogram; tss_schnorr_nonce_reuse_attempt_total counter (increment if Nonce already consumed; alert on any non-zero value).
  • Logs: DEBUG round1_commit signer_id={{id}} with commitment points as hex (public, safe to log); WARN IdentityCommitment/DuplicateSignerId with offending signer_id; ERROR nonce reuse detected with signer_id + session ID. No nonce scalar values (d_i, e_i) in any log or metric.
  • Nonce state stored in-memory only, not persisted across restarts; coordinator must re-collect commitments after any restart. RFC 9591 ciphersuite string must match exactly for cross-implementation compatibility; configurable at build time via feature flag (frost-secp256k1, frost-ed25519).

Phase 10 — FROST Partial Signing + Aggregation

10.1 Objectives

  • Complete the FROST round-2 signing flow in tss-schnorr: partial signature computation, aggregation, final verification, and identifiable abort.
  • Each signing party i computes z_i = d_i + rho_i*e_i + lambda_i * share_i * c using its Lagrange coefficient lambda_i over the signing set S.
  • Aggregator sums z = sum_{i in S}(z_i) and outputs signature (R, z).
  • Final verification: g^z == R + c*Y where Y is the joint DKG public key, c = H(R, Y, msg).
  • Per-partial verification: g^{z_i} == D_i + rho_i*E_i + c*lambda_i*Y_i to identify a misbehaving signer before aggregation (I-abort).
  • Enforce RFC 9591 wire format for (R, z) and for PartialSignature; cross-reference spine type names throughout.
  • Constraints: |S| >= t; signer ids must be a subset of those who committed in round 1; nonces consumed exactly once.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P5/P6/I7/ADR-18/ADR-20 — FROST partial sign + identifiable abort; S1 RFC 9591 §4.3; P4 lagrange constant-time

Module layout in tss-schnorr:

File Responsibility
src/round2.rs partial_sign — per-party computation of z_i
src/aggregate.rs aggregate — collect PartialSignature list, verify each, sum z
src/verify.rs verify_aggregate — final g^z == R + c*Y check
src/transcript.rs transcript binding: commitment list, signing set, message, partial sigs
src/identifiable_abort.rs identify_culprit — iterate partial sigs, return first failing ShareId
src/state.rs SignerState enum tracking round progression

partial_sign contract (src/round2.rs): fn partial_sign(state: &mut SignerState, signing_set: &[ShareId], msg: &[u8], commitment_list: &CommitmentList) -> Result<PartialSignature>. Steps: (1) assert state == Round1Done; (2) sort commitment_list by ShareId (deterministic rho_i); (3) recompute rho_i = H(i, msg, commitment_list) per RFC 9591 sec. 4.3; (4) recompute R = sum(D_j + rho_j*E_j); (5) c = H(R, Y, msg) via challenge hash; (6) lambda_i = lagrange_coefficient(i, signing_set) from tss-shamir; (7) z_i = d_i + rho_i*e_i + lambda_i * share * c; (8) zeroize d_i, e_i; (9) transition state -> Round2Done. Output: PartialSignature { signer_id: ShareId, z: Scalar }. aggregate contract (src/aggregate.rs): fn aggregate(partials: &[PartialSignature], commitment_list: &CommitmentList, public_shares: &PublicShareMap, Y: Point, msg: &[u8], params: &ThresholdParams) -> Result<AggregateSignature>. Preconditions: partials.len() >= params.t; signer ids distinct; all ids in commitment_list. Per-partial check for each (z_i, D_i, E_i, Y_i, lambda_i): verify g^{z_i} == D_i + rho_i*E_i + c*lambda_i*Y_i; on failure record i and continue, then return Err(IdentifiableAbort { culprit: ShareId }). Aggregate: z = sum(z_i) (field add); extract R from commitment-list computation. Output: AggregateSignature { R: Point, z: Scalar }. verify_aggregate (src/verify.rs): fn verify_aggregate(sig: &AggregateSignature, Y: Point, msg: &[u8]) -> Result<()>. Compute c = H(sig.R, Y, msg); check g * sig.z == sig.R + Y * c; Ok(()) or Err(VerificationFailed).

SignerState (src/state.rs):

enum SignerState { Round1Done(NonceState), Round2Done, Aborted(String) }   // NonceState holds (d_i,e_i,D_i,E_i)

Transition: Round1Done -> Round2Done on success, Round1Done -> Aborted on error; Round2Done/Aborted reject further partial_sign calls. NonceState holds nonces as Scalar fields; derives Zeroize; zeroize on Drop. CommitmentList is Vec<(ShareId, NonceCommitment)> sorted by ShareId, encapsulated in tss-schnorr/src/commitment_list.rs with a constructor that sorts on creation. Challenge hash c: domain-separated H("FROST-challenge" || encode(R) || encode(Y) || encode(msg)), RFC 9591 Appendix B compressed-point + length-prefixed-message encoding. Lagrange coefficient delegates to tss-shamir::lagrange::lagrange_coefficient(id: ShareId, set: &[ShareId]) -> Result<Scalar> — constant-time, tested separately (Phase 3).

10.3 Deliverable

  • partial_sign, aggregate, verify_aggregate compile; all RFC 9591 test vectors pass.
  • CLI tss-cli sign --threshold t --parties n runs a simulated n-party signing session; prints (R, z) in hex.
  • CLI tss-cli verify --pubkey Y --message msg --sig (R,z) returns exit 0 on valid.
  • identify_culprit returns the injected bad signer's ShareId in the injected-fault test.
  • SignerState machine prevents nonce reuse; second partial_sign call returns Err(NonceAlreadyConsumed).

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
RFC 9591 mandatory test vectors 100% exact match on (R, z)
Random (t,n) in [2,10], 1 000 trials all aggregates verify under joint key
Tampered z_i detected by per-partial verify 100% of injected faults identified
Culprit ShareId reported correctly matches injected signer id
Nonce reuse rejected Err(NonceAlreadyConsumed) every time
t-1 signers Err(BelowThreshold) before any computation
Duplicate signer ids in partials Err(DuplicateSignerId)

fixtures/frost/rfc9591_p256_vectors.json RFC 9591 P-256 mandatory exact (R, z)

10.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug / fix
Wrong Lagrange set aggregate fails to verify signing set mismatch between round-1 and round-2 callers bind signing set to transcript in round-1; assert equality in round-2
Nonce reuse partial nonce equation has two unknowns → solvable, share exposed d_i reused across sessions SignerState machine; zeroize on first use; NonceAlreadyConsumed
Unsorted commitment list rho_i differs per party non-deterministic insertion order sort CommitmentList by ShareId in constructor, not caller
Wrong challenge hash encoding RFC 9591 vector mismatch compressed vs. uncompressed point encoding lock to compressed encoding; document in transcript.rs
Missing per-partial verify bad signer undetected; final verify fails opaquely skipping I-abort check to save latency always run per-partial check; parallelize to keep latency low
Lagrange denominator zero, or sub-group attack on nonce commits panic/division by zero, or aggregate accepts forged sig duplicate ShareId in signing set; D_i/E_i low-order or identity reject duplicate ids Err(DuplicateSignerId); validate all commitment points non-identity and on-curve in round-1
Partial sig from non-participant aggregation accepts extra z_i aggregator does not check set membership filter partials to only ids in commitment_list

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • Per-partial verification: one scalar mul per signer, parallelized with rayon::par_iter over the partials slice. Lagrange coefficient: O(t) field muls per signer; precompute/cache if the signing set is stable across sessions.
  • CommitmentList sort: O(t log t), negligible, cache sorted form in the struct. Binding factor hash: one call per signer per round, batch if the hash function supports incremental hashing. z aggregation: one field add per signer, sequential fine (< 1 µs for t ≤ 100).
  • Memory: preallocate Vec<PartialSignature> with capacity: params.n; avoid Point (de)serialization in the hot path — pass by reference throughout.

10.8 Edge Cases

  • t = n: all parties must sign; any absent party causes Err(BelowThreshold) after timeout, no silent completion. t = 1: lambda_i = 1 for the sole signer, denominator is 1 (no inversion needed) — verify explicitly.
  • t = n = 1: degenerate single-party "threshold", effectively plain Schnorr — must still produce a valid (R, z) passing verify_aggregate. ShareId(u32::MAX) must be representable as a non-zero field element; validated on construction.
  • Message of length 0: H(R, Y, "") is well-defined, not special-cased, tested explicitly. Commitment list with a party not in the signing set is valid (committed but not signing, not rejected); signing set with a party who did not commit → Err(MissingCommitment).
  • Aggregator receives partial sigs from more than n parties: reject extras, use exactly the t..=n sigs from the committed set.

10.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection mechanism Blast radius Containment Recovery
Signer submits bad z_i per-partial verify in aggregate signing session blocked identify culprit; session aborted restart round1 without culprit; log IdentifiableAbort event
Aggregator falsifies R final verify_aggregate fails at verifiers all verifiers reject signature clients re-request from a different aggregator re-run with a different aggregator; audit aggregator logs
Transcript tampered transcript hash mismatch on verification session invalid all parties independently verify transcript restart session; investigate network path
KeyShare corruption (secret_share wrong) partial sig always fails per-partial check party cannot sign proactive recovery (Phase 12) DKG reshare to replace the corrupted share
Nonce pool exhaustion NonceAlreadyConsumed or pool empty party cannot enter round1 block round1 until refresh proactive refresh triggers new nonce precomputation; configurable low-watermark alert

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: tss_partial_sign_duration_ms/tss_aggregate_duration_ms/tss_verify_duration_ms (histograms), tss_iabort_total (counter), tss_nonce_pool_depth (gauge).
  • Structured log events: {event:"round2_partial_sign", signer_id, session_id, signing_set}, {event:"aggregate_ok", session_id, t, n}, {event:"identifiable_abort", culprit:ShareId, session_id}.
  • Trace: signing session as root span (session_id), child spans for partial_sign per party, aggregate, verify_aggregate. Nonce precomputation pool: configurable depth (default 100), alert when depth < 10, background task refills.
  • CLI sign --dry-run computes/verifies partial sigs without writing output (integration smoke test). RFC 9591 vector CI gate (cargo test --test rfc9591) is a required merge check; any nonzero tss_iabort_total triggers an operator alert and key-ceremony investigation.

Phase 11 — Threshold BLS

11.1 Objectives

  • Implement threshold BLS in tss-bls: partial sig sigma_i = H(m)^{share_i}; aggregate sigma = H(m)^{secret} from any t partials via Lagrange-in-exponent; verify via pairing: e(sigma, g) == e(H(m), Y).
  • Support two curve variants via feature flags: G1-sig/G2-key (default) and G2-sig/G1-key; treat the pairing backend as an explicit external dependency — without the pairing feature, expose a PhaseDeferred stub that compiles cleanly but returns Err(PhaseDeferredError) at runtime; also support per-partial-signature verification for identifiable abort: e(sigma_i, g) == e(H(m), Y_i).
  • Hash-to-curve follows RFC 9380 (hash_to_G1/hash_to_G2), domain-separated from FROST hashes.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P8/I6/ADR-21/ADR-22 — BLS12-381 threshold signatures; S2 RFC 9380 hash-to-curve + cofactor clearing; P4 lagrange-in-exponent

Module layout in tss-bls:

File Responsibility
src/lib.rs feature-gate: re-exports impl_bls or stub
src/partial.rs partial_sign_bls
src/aggregate.rs aggregate_bls — Lagrange in the exponent
src/verify.rs verify_bls — pairing check
src/partial_verify.rs verify_partial_bls — per-partial pairing check for I-abort
src/stub.rs PhaseDeferred stub
src/pairing.rs PairingEngine trait + default impl
src/hash_to_curve.rs RFC 9380 hash_to_g1, hash_to_g2 wrappers

PairingEngine trait (src/pairing.rs), default impl feature-gated on bls12-381 (alt: bn254); GtElement is an opaque struct from the pairing crate with derived PartialEq:

trait PairingEngine {
    fn pairing_g1_g2(a: &G1Point, b: &G2Point) -> GtElement;
    fn hash_to_g1(msg: &[u8], dst: &[u8]) -> G1Point;
    fn hash_to_g2(msg: &[u8], dst: &[u8]) -> G2Point;
}

Core functions:

  • partial_sign_bls (src/partial.rs): fn partial_sign_bls(share: &KeyShare, msg: &[u8], dst: &[u8]) -> Result<PartialSignature> — (1) h = hash_to_g1(msg, dst) (G1-sig variant); (2) sigma_i = share.secret_share * h (scalar mul on G1Point); (3) return PartialSignature { signer_id: share.id, sigma: sigma_i }. dst is the domain-separation tag, e.g. b"BLS-threshold-v1".
  • aggregate_bls (src/aggregate.rs): fn aggregate_bls(partials: &[PartialSignature], signing_set: &[ShareId]) -> Result<AggregateSignature>. Preconditions: partials.len() >= t; signer ids distinct. For each i in signing_set: lambda_i = lagrange_coefficient(i, signing_set) (tss-shamir); scalar-mul lambda_i * sigma_i on G1 and accumulate via point add — Lagrange interpolation in the exponent: sigma = sum(lambda_i * H(m)^{share_i}) = H(m)^{sum(lambda_i * share_i)} = H(m)^{secret}. Output: AggregateSignature { sigma: G1Point }.
  • verify_bls / verify_partial_bls (src/verify.rs, src/partial_verify.rs): verify_bls(sig, pubkey, msg, dst) computes h = hash_to_g1(msg, dst) and checks pairing(sig.sigma, g2_generator) == pairing(h, pubkey); verify_partial_bls(partial, public_share, msg, dst) runs the same check per-partial against public_share and is called inside aggregate_bls before accumulation to identify the first failing ShareId. Equality in GT; returns Ok(()) or Err(VerificationFailed).
  • Stub (src/stub.rs): all public functions return Err(TssError::PhaseDeferredError("pairing feature not enabled; rebuild with --features pairing")) and import no pairing crate, so the module compiles with zero feature deps.

Cargo.toml features:

[features]
default = ["g1-sig"]
pairing = ["dep:bls12-381"]
g1-sig = []
g2-sig = []

g1-sig and g2-sig are mutually exclusive; enforced via build.rs/cfg assert.

11.3 Deliverable

  • With --features pairing: partial_sign_bls, aggregate_bls, verify_bls compile and all tests pass; without it, the crate still compiles but runtime calls return Err(PhaseDeferredError).
  • CLI tss-cli sign --scheme bls and tss-cli verify --scheme bls wire through tss-cli.
  • Per-partial verify identifies injected bad sigma_i in the I-abort test.
  • Hash-to-curve output matches RFC 9380 test vectors.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Known-answer test (precomputed BLS aggregate, t=3, n=5) exact compressed G1 point match

Tampered sigma_i detected by verify_partial_bls 100% of injected faults
t-1 partial sigs Err(BelowThreshold)

11.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Fix
Cofactor not cleared after hash-to-curve point not in prime-order subgroup; pairing check fails cofactor multiplication skipped call G1Affine::clear_cofactor() inside hash_to_g1; test with subgroup check
Lagrange in wrong group type error or silent wrong result lambda_i (Scalar) applied to G2Point when G1-sig variant expected type-level enforcement: sigma_i: G1Point; lambda_i: Scalar
Pairing convention swapped e(g, sigma) != e(sigma, g) by asymmetry argument order wrong in pairing call lock convention in PairingEngine trait doc; test against precomputed GT value
Aggregation over duplicate signers wrong Lagrange, wrong sigma aggregator does not deduplicate validate distinct signer ids in aggregate_bls; Err(DuplicateSignerId)
BLS key reuse across signing protocols cross-protocol signing oracle no domain separation in hash use distinct dst per protocol; enforce dst is non-empty and protocol-specific
Small-subgroup attack on sigma_i adversary submits low-order point no subgroup check before aggregation validate sigma_i in prime-order subgroup before accumulation
Feature flag absent in production runtime panic instead of error stub panics instead of returning Err stub must return Err, never panic; test the no-feature path

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • Pairing is the dominant cost: one Miller loop ~3–8 ms on BLS12-381 per call (verify_bls calls pairing twice); batch multiple aggregate-sig verifications with multi_miller_loop to cut cost ~40%, and avoid redundant hash_to_g1 calls by computing h once per aggregate_bls call and reusing it in the verify_partial_bls loop.
  • Scalar mul for lambda_i * sigma_i: O(t) calls, parallelize with rayon::par_iter (~1–2 ms each); hash-to-curve is separately ~1–2 ms and must be constant-time (P4/I9).
  • G2-sig variant: G2 scalar mul is ~3× slower than G1 (trade-off vs. smaller public keys); serialized sigma is 48 bytes (G1 compressed) or 96 bytes (G2) — factor into wire-format planning.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • t = 1: lambda_i = 1, sigma = sigma_1, pairing verify still required (no shortcut); identity sigma_i submitted must be rejected before aggregation with Err(InvalidPoint) — do not propagate identity through pairing.
  • Empty signing_set: aggregate_bls returns Err(EmptySigningSet) immediately; message length 0: hash_to_g1(b"", dst) must succeed since hash-to-curve is defined on empty input — test explicitly.
  • ShareId values near p: must be representable as Scalar; validated on ShareId construction.
  • G2-sig and G1-sig features both enabled: build.rs asserts mutually exclusive and cargo build fails with a clear message; pairing feature enabled but crate absent from Cargo.lock surfaces as a compile-time link error, not a runtime one.

11.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Pairing backend C-FFI crash process abort / SIGABV signing session lost sandbox pairing calls in a subprocess or catch via std::panic::catch_unwind operator restart; log panic backtrace
Corrupt sigma_i from dishonest signer verify_partial_bls fails aggregate blocked identify, exclude, re-sign without culprit restart BLS signing round without culprit
Wrong joint public key in verify Err(VerificationFailed) on all valid sigs all sigs rejected clients detect mismatch re-derive Y from DKG epoch state; audit DKG output
Feature mismatch (pairing in test, not prod) prod returns PhaseDeferredError BLS unavailable in prod CI must run both feature permutations redeploy with pairing feature enabled
Hash-to-curve non-compliance with RFC 9380 interop failure with external BLS verifiers cross-system sig incompatibility pin RFC 9380 test vectors in CI fix dst or encoding; redeploy

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: tss_bls_partial_sign_ms (histogram), tss_bls_aggregate_ms, tss_bls_verify_ms, tss_bls_iabort_total (counter); structured log events: { event: "bls_partial_sign", signer_id, msg_hash_hex }, { event: "bls_aggregate_ok", t, n, session_id }, { event: "bls_identifiable_abort", culprit: ShareId }.
  • Trace: root span per BLS signing session; child spans for hash_to_g1, each partial_sign_bls, aggregate_bls, verify_bls.
  • Pairing latency spike (> 100 ms) indicates CPU throttling or a pairing backend regression — alert on tss_bls_verify_ms p99 > 100; likewise tss_bls_iabort_total > 0 should page on-call immediately, since a misbehaving BLS signer may indicate a key compromise or Byzantine party.
  • CLI error message when pairing feature absent: "BLS threshold signing requires the 'pairing' feature; rebuild with: cargo build --features tss-bls/pairing"; document the dst value in tss-cli's --help — it must match across all parties, or a mismatch causes silent verification failure.

Phase 12 — Proactive Secret Sharing

12.1 Objectives

  • Implement proactive refresh in tss-proactive: parties jointly produce a new valid sharing of the same secret by sharing zero and adding the refresh deltas to existing shares.
  • Enforce epoch semantics (old shares invalidated after refresh completes; an attacker must compromise t parties within one epoch to recover the secret, P7) and implement lost-share recovery — a party that lost its share recovers it from t helpers via Lagrange interpolation.
  • Integrate with KeyShare and tss-dkg state (epoch counter increments; group public key unchanged, I8), verifying refresh deltas via Feldman VSS from tss-vss without a trusted coordinator.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P7/P9/I8/ADR-24 — proactive refresh; zero-polynomial invariant; epoch model; Herzberg 1995 mobile adversary bound

Module layout in tss-proactive:

File Responsibility
src/refresh.rs generate_refresh_poly, apply_refresh, finalize_refresh
src/recovery.rs recover_share — Lagrange from t helpers
src/epoch.rs EpochState struct; epoch transition validation
src/zero_poly.rs helper: sample degree-(t-1) polynomial with constant term 0
src/verify_delta.rs Feldman check for refresh sub-shares

Core functions:

  • generate_refresh_poly (src/refresh.rs): fn generate_refresh_poly(params: &ThresholdParams, rng: &mut dyn CryptoRng) -> Result<(Polynomial, Commitment)> — (1) sample coeffs[1..t] uniformly; (2) set coeffs[0] = Scalar::zero() (constant term is exactly zero); (3) compute Feldman commitment C_j = g^{coeffs[j]}; (4) return (Polynomial { coeffs }, Commitment { points }). Assert poly.coeffs[0] == Scalar::zero() before returning; zeroize coeffs on drop via Zeroize.
  • distribute_refresh_shares (inline in refresh.rs): fn distribute_refresh_shares(poly: &Polynomial, parties: &[ShareId]) -> Vec<Share> — evaluate poly at each ShareId (Horner's method, reuse tss-shamir::eval); zeroize the polynomial after evaluation.
  • apply_refresh (src/refresh.rs): fn apply_refresh(share: &mut KeyShare, received_deltas: &[(ShareId, Share, Commitment)]) -> Result<()> — for each (dealer_id, delta_j, commitment), call tss-vss::feldman::verify_share(delta_j, commitment, share.id) and reject Err(CommitmentMismatch); new share scalar share.secret_share += sum(delta_j.value); new public share share.public_share += sum(commitment[0]) — since each commitment[0] = g^0 = identity, the sum is identity and public_share is unchanged (assert this invariant); increment share.epoch; zeroize all delta_j.value intermediates.
  • finalize_refresh (src/refresh.rs): fn finalize_refresh(all_constant_term_commits: &[Point]) -> Result<()> — sum all commitment[0] points and assert the sum equals the group identity (Point::identity()), returning Err(RefreshInvariantViolation) if not; this ensures the secret is preserved (I8): sum(delta_i(0)) = 0 in the group.
  • recover_share (src/recovery.rs): fn recover_share(party_id: ShareId, helpers: &[(ShareId, Share)], params: &ThresholdParams, public_share: &Point) -> Result<Share> — preconditions: helpers.len() >= params.t, all helper ids distinct and valid; Lagrange-interpolate at party_id via tss-shamir::reconstruct_at(helpers, party_id) (new function evaluating the interpolating polynomial at an arbitrary point, not just x=0); validate g^{recovered.value} == public_share, rejecting on mismatch; return Share { id: party_id, value: recovered }.
  • reconstruct_at addition to tss-shamir (tss-shamir/src/reconstruct.rs): fn reconstruct_at(shares: &[Share], x: ShareId) -> Result<Scalar> — Lagrange interpolation at arbitrary x, reusing the coefficient computation from Phase 3; constant-time, zeroizes intermediate scalars.

EpochState (src/epoch.rs), persisted to disk as CBOR, loaded on startup, updated atomically (write to temp file, rename); epoch is validated in apply_refresh (reject if received_epoch != current_epoch + 1):

struct EpochState {
    epoch: u64,
    params: ThresholdParams,
    group_public: Point,
    committed_parties: Vec<ShareId>,
}

RefreshRound message types added to tss-protocol/src/messages.rs: RefreshCommit { epoch, dealer_id, commitment } (broadcast); RefreshShare { epoch, dealer_id, recipient_id, delta } (unicast, encrypted); RefreshFinalize { epoch, party_id, ack } (broadcast after applying).

12.3 Deliverable

  • generate_refresh_poly, apply_refresh, finalize_refresh compile and pass correctness tests.
  • recover_share restores a lost share and validates against the public share.
  • CLI tss-cli refresh simulates a full proactive refresh over n parties, outputting new key_share.bin files with incremented epoch (the KeyShare epoch counter increments after each successful refresh).
  • Sign with refreshed shares; verify under the original joint public key: passes.
  • tss-shamir::reconstruct_at implemented and tested independently.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Secret unchanged after refresh (sign pre/post, verify under same Y) passes for ≥ 500 random (t,n) trials
Refreshed shares (any t-subset) reconstruct original secret ≥ 500 random trials
finalize_refresh constant-term sum == identity verified in every refresh cycle
Lost share recovery (t helpers, valid) recovered.value == original.value; g^{recovered} == Y_i
Tampered delta commitment detected Err(CommitmentMismatch)
Stale epoch share rejected Err(StaleEpoch)
Zeroization: no secret bytes after refresh miri / valgrind scan passes
Epoch state survives crash + reload EpochState round-trip via CBOR
Under-threshold recovery helpers Err(BelowThreshold)

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected
fixtures/proactive/refresh_t3n5.json full refresh, 3-of-5 new shares reconstruct same secret; epoch = old+1
fixtures/proactive/tampered_delta_commit.json party 2 sends bad commitment[1] Err(CommitmentMismatch) with dealer_id=2
fixtures/proactive/nonzero_constant_term.json party 1 uses coeffs[0] = 1 finalize_refresh returns Err(RefreshInvariantViolation)

fixtures/proactive/epoch_state_roundtrip.json serialize then deserialize EpochState identical struct

12.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Fix
Refresh polynomial constant term != 0 finalize_refresh fails; secret changes coeffs[0] not zeroed assert coeffs[0] == Scalar::zero() in generate_refresh_poly; enforce in type
Old share not invalidated epoch bypass; stale share accepted KeyShare.epoch not checked in signing ops enforce epoch check at every signing entry point; Err(StaleEpoch)
Malicious party uses nonzero delta constant secret biased finalize_refresh not called always call finalize_refresh before committing new shares; abort entire epoch on failure
Recovery share leaked to non-recovering party confidentiality lost plaintext share in protocol message mandate AEAD encryption on RefreshShare messages; never log share bytes
Partial refresh epoch (k < n parties complete) inconsistent shares across parties network partition during refresh abort epoch; all parties retain old shares; restart with same epoch id after reconnect
Zeroize elided by compiler secret persists in memory dead-store optimization use zeroize crate with Zeroize derive; #[must_use] on zeroize calls; test with miri
reconstruct_at called with x not in share-id space wrong interpolation x treated as arbitrary scalar validate x is a valid ShareId; x != 0 (0 is the secret itself)
Concurrent refresh and signing corrupt intermediate share no protocol-level lock state machine: EpochTransitioning state blocks signing; enforce in tss-protocol

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Refresh polynomial generation: O(t) scalar samples + O(t) scalar muls for Feldman commitment; per-party cost matches original VSS (Phase 6).
  • apply_refresh (O(n) field additions) and finalize_refresh (O(n) point adds) are both negligible per party (< 1 µs for n ≤ 100).
  • Recovery (reconstruct_at): O(t) field muls for Lagrange coefficients + O(t) field muls for evaluation; single scalar mul for validation.
  • Network: O(n^2) unicast messages per refresh epoch (each of n parties sends to n-1 recipients); document; optimize with a broadcast tree for large n.
  • Epoch state persistence: one CBOR write per refresh; use O_SYNC or fsync to avoid data loss; measure latency.
  • Avoid holding Polynomial in memory longer than necessary; drop and zeroize immediately after evaluation.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • n = t: all n parties must participate; one absent party blocks epoch; define a liveness timeout after which the epoch is aborted and retried.
  • epoch counter at u64::MAX returns Err(EpochOverflow) before incrementing; refresh with different ThresholdParams (new t or n) is resharing (Phase 4), not proactive refresh — reject if params != current_params.
  • ShareId not in the original party set: reject in distribute_refresh_shares (Lagrange at an unregistered point is undefined); recovery party id = 0 is also rejected since ShareId(0) is the secret itself (x=0), Err(InvalidShareId).
  • Party applies refresh delta twice (duplicate message): idempotent check — if share.epoch == received_epoch - 1, apply; if share.epoch == received_epoch, reject Err(AlreadyApplied).
  • Zero scalar after refresh (astronomically rare): share value = Scalar::zero() is technically valid but cryptographically degenerate; detect and abort with Err(DegenerateShare).

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Refresh aborted mid-epoch finalize_refresh not called; RefreshFinalize acks missing parties hold inconsistent partial updates discard partial deltas; restore from pre-refresh KeyShare backup; restart epoch operator triggers retry with same epoch id
Malicious party biases secret finalize_refresh detects non-identity sum epoch aborted; secret preserved identify culprit (their commitment[0] != identity); exclude retry without culprit; record in audit log
Lost share: < t helpers online Err(BelowThreshold) party offline for signing wait for more helpers; alert operator bring more parties online or trigger DKG reshare
Epoch state file corrupted CBOR deserialization error on startup party cannot participate recover from backup; if no backup, DKG reshare DKG reshare from surviving parties
Memory side-channel of old share timing analysis reveals share bits epoch isolation broken constant-time zeroize (zeroize crate); miri; P4/I9 immediate epoch refresh; security incident
Concurrent signing + refresh signing uses stale share mid-refresh signature may be invalid EpochTransitioning state blocks signing in tss-protocol state machine fix; replay signing after refresh

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: tss_refresh_epoch_total (counter), tss_refresh_duration_ms (histogram), tss_recovery_attempts_total, tss_recovery_success_total, tss_stale_share_rejections_total, tss_epoch_transition_blocked_ms; structured log events: { event: "refresh_start", epoch, n, t }, { event: "refresh_delta_applied", party_id, epoch }, { event: "refresh_finalized", epoch }, { event: "refresh_invariant_violation", culprit, epoch }, { event: "share_recovery_ok", party_id, epoch }.
  • Trace: root span per refresh epoch, child spans per party's apply_refresh, finalize_refresh as terminal span; EpochState persistence writes to epoch_state.bin.tmp then atomically renames to epoch_state.bin, logging a checksum of written state.
  • CLI tss-cli refresh --epoch-duration 86400 sets epoch duration in seconds; default 86400 (24 h); configurable at runtime without rebuild.
  • Alert policy: tss_stale_share_rejections_total > 0 — indicates a party running stale software or under attack; page on-call immediately.
  • Recommend a dedicated background service to trigger refresh at epoch boundary rather than ad-hoc operator invocation; snapshot key_share.bin and epoch_state.bin before each refresh and retain for at least two epochs to allow rollback.

Phase 13 — Protocol Orchestration + CLI

13.1 Objectives

  • Implement tss-protocol: typed round messages, transcript binding all public values, identifiable-abort, timeout detection.
  • Implement tss-cli: subcommands split | reconstruct | dkg | sign | verify | refresh over a simulated in-process network.
  • Wire all prior crates (tss-shamir, tss-vss, tss-dkg, tss-schnorr, tss-bls, tss-proactive) behind a single ergonomic entry point.
  • Constraints: no real network I/O; simulated multi-party execution in a single process; JSON or binary serializable messages; all public values bound in a transcript before signing.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P5/P6/P4/I6/ADR-25/ADR-30 — protocol layer; Fiat-Shamir transcript binding; identifiable abort state machine; session-id prevents replay

tss-protocol crate

Module Responsibility
message.rs RoundMessage enum: DkgCommit, DkgShare, DkgComplaint, DkgFinalize, FrostCommit, FrostPartial, BlsPartial, RefreshCommit, RefreshShare
transcript.rs Transcript struct wrapping a hash accumulator; append_point, append_scalar, append_message, challenge_scalar — binds session id, participants, message, all commitments before producing the Fiat-Shamir challenge
session.rs Session<State> — generic state machine over DkgRound / FROST round enums; advance(msg) -> Result<Transition>
abort.rs IdentifiableAbort — records offending ShareId, evidence (bad commitment, invalid partial sig, conflicting shares), formats a blame proof; verify_blame(blame, public_state)
timeout.rs RoundDeadline — per-round tick counter; is_expired(current_tick) -> bool; sessions that expire emit SessionError::Timeout(round)
network_sim.rs SimNetwork — maps ShareId -> mpsc channel; broadcast, send_to, collect_round(round_id, expected_count, deadline)

tss-cli crate

Subcommand Inputs Behaviour
split --secret hex --t N --n N --out dir calls tss-shamir::split, writes JSON share files
reconstruct --shares dir --t N loads share files, calls tss-shamir::reconstruct, prints hex secret
dkg --n N --t N spawns n Party threads on SimNetwork, runs full DKG, writes key-share-{i}.json
sign --key-shares dir --msg hex --scheme frost|bls runs FROST round 1+2 or BLS partial+aggregate, prints signature
verify --pub-key hex --sig hex --msg hex --scheme frost|bls calls scheme verifier, exits 0/1
refresh --key-shares dir runs proactive refresh via tss-proactive, overwrites key-share files

CLI argument parsing: clap derive. Error output on stderr; machine-readable JSON with --json flag. Key-share files: serde_json serializing KeyShare struct. Transcript initialized from --session-id (default: random 32-byte hex).

13.3 Deliverable

  • tss-protocol compiles; Session state machine accepts well-formed messages, rejects out-of-order or duplicate messages.
  • tss-cli binary builds; all six subcommands execute end-to-end on localhost simulation; split/reconstruct round-trips; DKG produces consistent KeyShare files; FROST sign+verify succeeds; BLS sign+verify succeeds (or prints PhaseDeferred if pairing unavailable).
  • cargo run -- dkg --n 5 --t 3 && cargo run -- sign --scheme frost completes without error.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Test Pass Condition
test_session_roundtrip_dkg 5-party DKG simulation completes in < 500 ms, all 5 KeyShare joint pubkeys equal
test_frost_sign_verify_cli FROST sign + verify over simulated 3-of-5 returns exit code 0
test_identifiable_abort inject bad share from party 2; blame proof identifies ShareId(2)
test_timeout_triggers stall one party's channel; SessionError::Timeout raised within deadline ticks
test_transcript_binding two sessions with different msgs produce different challenges
test_split_reconstruct_cli split then reconstruct recovers exact secret for t in {2,3,5}

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/proto/dkg_5_3_transcript.json 5-party DKG transcript + final pubkey joint pubkey bytes stable across runs
fixtures/proto/frost_session_3_5.json complete FROST session messages + signature matches Phase 10 conformance output
fixtures/proto/blame_bad_share.json injected invalid share from party 3 IdentifiableAbort { culprit: ShareId(3) }
fixtures/proto/timeout_round2.json round-2 message never arrives SessionError::Timeout(FrostRound::Partial)
fixtures/proto/transcript_collision.json two sessions, same parties, different msgs distinct Fiat-Shamir challenges

13.6 Pitfalls

  • Message replay across sessions: a message from session A accepted in session B if session-id not included in transcript. Bind session-id as first transcript entry.
  • Out-of-order message acceptance: Session::advance must buffer future-round messages or reject them; silent acceptance corrupts state.
  • Transcript non-determinism: floating-point or HashMap ordering in commitment list causes non-reproducible challenges. Sort ShareId ascending before appending to transcript.
  • SimNetwork deadlock: symmetric send_to on same thread blocks if channel buffer full. Use try_send with back-pressure error rather than unbounded channels.
  • Blame proof forgery: a party can claim another misbehaved. verify_blame must re-derive the expected commitment from the public state and confirm mismatch — never trust the accuser's copy.
  • JSON key-share file leakage: KeyShare.secret_share is a Scalar; serializing to world-readable files is a misuse. serde impl must gate on explicit feature flag insecure-serialize.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • SimNetwork::collect_round busy-polls channels; replace with select! or a condvar barrier to avoid CPU waste in multi-party simulation.
  • Transcript accumulator: hash-state clone per challenge call; clone is cheap but avoid cloning inside hot loops (nonce commitment aggregation in FROST round 1).
  • serde_json for key-share files adds ~50 µs per file; negligible for CLI but document that production deployments use binary encoding.
  • Clap argument parsing adds ~2 ms startup; acceptable for CLI, irrelevant for library users.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • --t > number of provided share files: must reject with ThresholdError::InsufficientShares before attempting reconstruction.
  • Party drops mid-DKG after round 1 commit but before round 2 share: qualified set must exclude it; remaining honest parties must complete if qualified set size >= t.
  • Duplicate --shares file paths: deduplicate by ShareId; two files for same id → DuplicateShareId error.
  • --msg empty or zero-length: permissible; transcript must still bind it (length-prefixed).
  • --json output flag with non-UTF8 binary: base64-encode all byte fields.
  • refresh with fewer key-share files than t: must abort before modifying any file.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Corrupt key-share file serde_json parse error at load single subcommand fails restore from backup; re-run DKG
Party crashes mid-DKG Timeout on missing messages session aborts restart DKG; prior key material unused
Bad partial sig not caught aggregate fails verify wrong/invalid signature returned --verify step catches it; identifiable-abort in subsequent run with logging
Transcript divergence final challenges differ across parties FROST aggregation fails enable RUST_LOG=tss_protocol=debug to compare per-party transcript states

13.10 Operational Notes

  • RUST_LOG=tss_protocol=debug logs per-party state transitions, round ids, message hashes.
  • RUST_LOG=tss_cli=info logs subcommand, party count, elapsed time per round.
  • Key-share files: document that rotating them requires refresh not manual edit.
  • --session-id flag: operators must supply a unique id per signing session; collisions cause transcript binding failures silently masked by different messages producing different challenges.
  • Metrics (counter): tss_session_rounds_total{scheme, outcome}, tss_abort_total{culprit_id}.

Phase 14 — Hardening + Conformance + Constant-Time + Fuzz + Packing

14.1 Objectives

  • Conformance: verify Shamir reconstruct correctness over random (t,n)/subset combinations; FROST against RFC 9591 test vectors; VSS soundness (tampered share fails); DKG joint-key correctness.
  • Constant-time audit: all share/nonce/field-inverse paths must be data-independent; enforce via subtle crate; CI gate with cargo-careful or MIRI.
  • Fuzz: malformed shares, commitments, partial signatures — no panic, no undefined behaviour, no wrong result. Pack: publish-ready crate hygiene; #[deny(unsafe_code)] enforcement; no_std compatibility audit; semver baseline; documentation coverage.

14.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: P4/P5/I9/ADR-27/ADR-28 — CT audit via dudect; MIRI for tss-field/tss-shamir; RFC 9591 conformance vectors; fuzz harness; S1/S2

Conformance suite (tests/conformance/)

File Scheme Source Validation
rfc9591_frost_*.json FROST/Schnorr RFC 9591 Appendix round-trip test vectors, signature bytes exact
shamir_random_tn.csv Shamir SSS generated 1000 random (t,n,secret,subset) rows
vss_feldman_tamper.json Feldman VSS constructed tampered share index, expected Err(VssError::VerifyFailed)
dkg_joint_key.json DKG generated n-party session, assert joint pubkey = product of constant commitments
bls_partial_vectors.json BLS threshold Drand-compatible partial sigs + aggregate, pairing verify

Constant-time audit

  • All Scalar arithmetic: implemented via subtle::Choice, subtle::ConstantTimeEq, subtle::ConditionallySelectable.
  • lagrange_coefficient (tss-shamir/tss-schnorr): inner loop uses field mul/inv, no branching on share values.
  • share_verify (tss-vss): comparison g^s == product C_j^{i^j} via ConstantTimeEq on Point repr.
  • FROST nonce handling (tss-schnorr): d_i, e_i zeroized via zeroize::Zeroize on drop.
  • CI gate: cargo careful test --all-features 2>&1 | grep -i "undefined behavior" must be clean; MIRIFLAGS="-Zmiri-disable-isolation" cargo miri test on tss-field, tss-shamir, tss-vss.

Fuzz targets (fuzz/fuzz_targets/)

Target Corpus Input Invariant Tested
fuzz_share_reconstruct arbitrary Vec<Share> bytes no panic; result is Ok or typed Err
fuzz_feldman_verify arbitrary commitment + share no panic; wrong share → Err, never Ok with wrong secret
fuzz_frost_partial arbitrary PartialSignature bytes aggregate never returns Ok on invalid partial
fuzz_dkg_message arbitrary RoundMessage bytes Session::advance returns typed error, no panic
fuzz_bls_aggregate arbitrary Vec<PartialSignature> no panic; pairing verify fails on garbage

Packing / crate hygiene

  • #[deny(unsafe_code)] on all crates; exceptions documented in safety.md inline.
  • no_std compatibility: tss-core, tss-field, tss-shamir, tss-vss, tss-group compile with default-features = false, features = ["no_std"]; allocator required via alloc.
  • Cargo.toml workspace: [workspace.metadata.docs.rs] set; cargo doc --no-deps zero warnings.
  • CHANGELOG.md: semver 0.1.0 baseline; API surface documented.
  • deny.toml: cargo deny check licenses advisories passes; only approved SPDX identifiers (MIT, Apache-2.0, BSD-3-Clause).

14.3 Deliverable

  • All conformance tests pass against RFC 9591 vectors (exact byte match for FROST).
  • 1000-row Shamir CSV conformance suite passes with zero failures.
  • MIRI clean on tss-field and tss-shamir.
  • cargo fuzz run fuzz_share_reconstruct -- -max_total_time=300 produces no crashes.
  • All crates compile with no_std (alloc).
  • cargo doc zero warnings; cargo deny check passes.

RFC 9591 FROST vectors all test-vector outputs byte-exact

VSS tamper test 100% tampered shares return Err(VssError::VerifyFailed)

14.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Source Validation Method
fixtures/rfc9591/frost-ed25519-sha512-*.json RFC 9591 Appendix A byte-compare signature (R‖z)
fixtures/rfc9591/frost-p256-sha256-*.json RFC 9591 Appendix B byte-compare
fixtures/shamir/random_tn_1000.csv generated by gen_fixtures binary reconstruct == secret for each row
fixtures/vss/feldman_tamper_cases.json constructed bad shares (value±1, id±1, swapped) all return Err
fixtures/dkg/joint_key_n5_t3.json 5-party DKG session log joint pubkey bytes stable
fixtures/bls/drand_partial_sigs.json Drand-compatible vectors pairing verify passes

14.6 Pitfalls

  • RFC 9591 hash domain separation: FROST uses specific DST strings for hash-to-scalar; using a wrong or missing DST produces valid-looking but wrong output that fails vector comparison silently if test does not compare intermediate hashes.
  • MIRI false negatives on constant-time: MIRI checks memory safety, not timing; constant-time property is not verified by MIRI alone. Use subtle crate primitives + manual audit + documentation that formal CT verification (ct-verif / binsec) is out of scope but recommended.
  • Fuzz corpus starvation: without a seed corpus, coverage-guided fuzzing stalls on length-prefixed formats. Provide seed corpus from valid fixtures.
  • no_std breakage from transitive dependencies: a dependency pulling std breaks no_std silently unless CI builds with cargo build --target thumbv7em-none-eabi or equivalent bare-metal target.
  • Semver hazard on type aliases: if Scalar type alias changes its underlying type between versions, downstream crates break without a major version bump. Lock underlying type in docs.
  • Fixture staleness: generated fixtures (Shamir CSV) must be reproducibly generated from a seeded PRNG; unseeded generation produces different expected values across CI runs.

14.7 Performance Concerns

  • MIRI runs 10–100× slower than native; limit MIRI targets to tss-field and tss-shamir; exclude tss-group (EC scalar mul is prohibitively slow under MIRI).
  • Fuzz build (-C opt-level=3 -C sanitize=address) adds ~30% compile time; run fuzz in a separate CI job.
  • Conformance CSV 1000-row test: Lagrange interpolation per row is O(t²); for t=20 this is fast (< 1 µs each), total < 100 ms.
  • cargo doc --all-features pulls in pairing crate (~60 s compile spike, cache with sccache in CI); cargo deny license check is near-instant — run early in CI to fail fast.

14.8 Edge Cases

  • RFC 9591 test vectors include edge cases: signer index 1 only, maximum n signers, message = empty bytes — all must pass.
  • Shamir conformance: t=1 (trivially any one share reconstructs); t=n (all shares required); n=1 (degenerate single-party).
  • VSS tamper: commitment list length mismatch (fewer commitments than degree+1) must return Err, not a wrong-degree polynomial evaluation.
  • Fuzz: zero-length byte input; maximum-length (64 KB) byte input; all-zero share value (valid but unusual); ShareId=0 (must be rejected — secret is at x=0).
  • no_std with global allocator panicking on OOM: crate must not leak or double-free; zeroize on drop is still called.
  • BLS vectors with PhaseDeferred pairing: test suite must skip (not fail) if pairing feature is disabled; CI job matrix includes features = ["pairing"].

14.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
RFC 9591 vector mismatch test assertion on byte comparison FROST scheme unusable in production audit DST strings, hash input encoding, scalar encoding endianness
MIRI UB in tss-field MIRI reports use of uninitialized memory entire field arithmetic suspect isolate to specific operation; replace with subtle-safe primitive
Fuzz crash (panic) libfuzzer exit code non-zero correctness invariant violated minimize corpus input; add regression test; fix before release
no_std compile failure CI bare-metal target fails embedded/no_std users blocked audit new dependency additions against no_std compatibility
License violation cargo deny advisory hit legal/distribution risk pin to prior version; find alternative dependency
Fixture staleness (wrong expected) conformance test fails on CI after fixture regen false CI failures pin fixture generation to seeded PRNG; commit generated fixtures

14.10 Operational Notes

  • CI matrix: stable, beta, nightly (nightly required for MIRI); separate jobs for fuzz (nightly + libfuzzer), MIRI (nightly), conformance (stable).
  • Fuzz artifacts (corpus/, artifacts/) excluded from crate publish via .gitignore + Cargo.toml exclude list.
  • Conformance fixture generation: cargo run --bin gen_fixtures -- --seed 0xdeadbeef produces deterministic CSV/JSON; committed to repo.
  • cargo audit in CI; cargo-semver-checks on every PR touching public API.
  • Release checklist: MIRI clean; fuzz 300 s per target clean; cargo deny clean; RFC 9591 vectors pass; cargo publish --dry-run succeeds for all crates in dependency order.
  • RUST_LOG=tss_conformance=info prints per-vector pass/fail with vector id for triage.

Threat Model

Trust Boundaries

TRUST ZONES (from the architecture diagram):
  Zone A — tss-cli / caller process         : untrusted input origin
  Zone B — tss-protocol / round messages    : semi-trusted (authenticated but unverified party)
  Zone C — tss-vss / tss-dkg internals      : trusted computation on locally-held shares
  Zone D — tss-field / tss-group primitives : trusted math (no I/O)
  Zone E — OS / CSPRNG                      : trusted platform assumption

Boundaries:
  A→B  : user-supplied JSON / binary messages; full validation at entry
  B→C  : per-message commitment checks before any share is used
  C→D  : field/group inputs must be validated (range, point on curve) before ops
  D→E  : entropy sourced from OS CSPRNG; auditable seeding path

Threat Table

ID Threat Component Attacker Goal Mitigation Detection Telemetry Recovery
T1 Malicious dealer sends inconsistent shares tss-vss Corrupt a recipient's share without detection Feldman/Pedersen commitment verification on every received share (I5) Commitment check fails → disqualification broadcast DKG complaint event with dealer id Complaint round removes dealer from qualified set
T2 Rogue-nonce / Wagner attack on FROST tss-schnorr Forge aggregate signature with < t parties Binding factor rho_i = H(i, msg, all-commitments) per P5; re-randomize each round Verification of (R,z) fails under joint key Signing abort + offending party id Restart signing session; discard all nonces
T3 Under-threshold reconstruction attempt tss-shamir Recover secret from < t shares I10: reconstruct() returns Err when shares < t Error path; no partial result emitted
T4 Duplicate or zero ShareId injection tss-core Cause Lagrange singularity / undefined interpolation ShareId(0) rejected; duplicate ids rejected in reconstruct (I10); set membership check Error on input validation Error counter: shamir.dupId, shamir.zeroId Caller must provide distinct non-zero ids
T5 Malformed / off-curve EC point tss-group Trigger undefined group ops or small-subgroup attack Point deserialization validates curve equation + cofactor check; reject identity unless explicitly valid Deserialization error Error counter: group.pointDeserFail Reject message; abort protocol round
T6 Out-of-range scalar injection tss-field / tss-group Wrap scalar arithmetic; leak share Reduce mod p on deserialization; reject zero scalar for share values Range check error Error counter: field.outOfRange Reject; request re-send
T7 Partial-signature forgery (FROST) tss-schnorr Pass off bad z_i as valid Partial-sig verification: g^{z_i} == D_i * rho_i*E_i * Y_i^c before aggregating (P6) Partial check failure → identifiable abort frost.partialFail{signer_id} Exclude signer; restart with qualified set
T8 Partial-signature forgery (BLS) tss-bls Inject invalid sigma_i Pairing check per partial sig: e(sigma_i, g) == e(H(m), Y_i) Pairing check failure bls.partialFail{signer_id} Exclude signer; reaggregate over remaining
T9 Nonce reuse across FROST sessions tss-schnorr Recover share from two equations with same nonce Nonces zeroized after use (P9); session transcript binds nonces to (msg, commitment-list) Transcript duplicate detection frost.nonceReuse alert Reject duplicate session id; rotate keys if confirmed
T10 Share leakage via timing side-channel tss-field / tss-schnorr Recover share from timing Constant-time field ops (P4, I9); Montgomery ladder for scalar mul; no secret-dependent branches Audit via cargo careful / dudect harness N/A (static analysis) Re-audit on every dependency update
T11 Memory disclosure (heap dump / core dump) tss-core, all secret holders Extract shares or nonces post-use zeroize crate on all Scalar / Share / nonce fields; disable core dump in CLI OS-level coredump suppression Startup log: coredump disabled Rotate shares if disclosure confirmed
T12 Malicious participant in DKG complaint round tss-dkg Frame honest parties with false complaints Complaints must include the allegedly bad share; verifier re-checks commitment; dishonest complainant disqualified Complaint re-verification fails dkg.falseComplaint{from} Disqualify complainant; continue with qualified set
T13 Supply-chain compromise of dependency tss-group / tss-bls Inject backdoored EC or pairing impl Pin exact dep versions in Cargo.lock; cargo-audit in CI; prefer audited crates (dalek, blst) cargo audit alert CI gate failure Downgrade dep; re-audit; rebuild
T14 Resource exhaustion (large n, many DKG messages) tss-dkg / tss-protocol DoS via O(n²) message flood ThresholdParams bounds (n ≤ 255 default); message-count limit per round Round message counter exceeds cap dkg.msgFlood{round} Drop excess messages; operator adjustable cap
T15 Operator misconfiguration (t > n, t = 0) tss-core Unusable or trivially broken sharing ThresholdParams::new() validates 1 ≤ t ≤ n; Err on violation Validation error at construction Error log: params.invalid Return error; user must correct params
T16 Transcript manipulation / replay tss-protocol Replay old partial sigs in new session Session-id + round number in transcript; binding over all prior messages; replayed sig has wrong challenge c Transcript hash mismatch proto.replayDetected{session} Abort session; generate new session-id
T17 State persistence corruption (CLI saved shares) tss-cli Corrupt stored shares to prevent reconstruction Shares encrypted at rest; stored with MAC; version + checksum header MAC verification on load cli.shareLoadFail Restore from backup; proactive refresh
T18 Proactive refresh epoch race tss-proactive Refresh with stale/mixed-epoch shares Epoch counter in share metadata; cross-epoch mixes rejected Epoch mismatch error refresh.epochMismatch Resync epoch; operator manually triggers re-refresh

Failure Modes & Recovery

Failure Taxonomy

Class Symptoms Root Causes Detection Auto Recovery Manual Recovery Operator Action Forensic Artifacts Long-term Mitigation
F1 — Under-threshold reconstruction Err(ThresholdNotMet) from reconstruct() Fewer than t valid shares presented; share corruption I10 check at reconstruct entry None (information-theoretic limit) Locate additional valid shares; restore from backup Audit who holds missing shares; verify share integrity Error log with share count and ids presented Proactive refresh with share redundancy; n > t+1
F2 — Share corruption / tampered value VSS commitment check fails for share i Bit-flip in storage; malicious holder; transmission error Feldman/Pedersen commitment verify (I5) Exclude share; continue if remaining ≥ t Re-derive share via proactive recovery (Phase 12); fetch from backup Mark holder i suspect; initiate share recovery Commitment check failure log with share id Authenticated storage (MAC); checksummed share files
F3 — Duplicate ShareId Err(DuplicateShareId) at reconstruct Buggy share assignment; replay of old share Input validation before Lagrange None Re-issue shares with distinct ids Audit share generation log Validation error log with duplicate id ShareId uniqueness enforced at split time; audit DKG assignment
F4 — Off-curve point in commitment Err(InvalidPoint) during VSS verify Corrupted commitment wire format; malicious dealer Point deserialization validates curve equation Disqualify dealer in DKG complaint round Re-run DKG excluding bad dealer Broadcast complaint; wait for qualified set Deserialization error + dealer id Pin dep versions; fuzz commitment parsing
F5 — FROST nonce exhaustion / reuse Signing session fails or produces invalid sig Nonce state not zeroized; state file corruption Session transcript detects duplicate nonce commitment Abort signing session Generate fresh nonces; restart round 1 Revoke signing session; rotate key shares if confirmed reuse frost.nonceReuse alert; transcript log Nonces generated per-session with OS entropy; never persisted
F6 — DKG complaint round deadlock DKG never reaches Finalize state Too many complaints; Byzantine minority > f Round timeout; DkgRound stuck at Complaint Timeout triggers identifiable abort Manually disqualify confirmed bad dealers; restart DKG with qualified set Audit complaint evidence; decide qualification DKG transcript with all complaints and responses Threshold design: n ≥ 2t+1 for robustness; timeout tuning
F7 — Partial signature aggregation failure (FROST) Err(AggregationFailed) or invalid (R,z) One or more bad z_i; wrong Lagrange set Partial-sig verify before aggregation (P6) Exclude bad signer; retry with remaining ≥ t Identify and replace misbehaving signer Trigger identifiable abort; exclude signer from session Bad signer id; partial sig values for audit Per-partial-sig verification mandatory before aggregate
F8 — BLS pairing check failure e(sigma, g) ≠ e(H(m), Y) at verification Wrong key material; corrupted sigma; wrong msg Pairing equation check (standard) None (signature invalid) Re-sign with correct key shares Audit key share provenance Failed pairing inputs logged Partial-sig verification per signer before aggregation
F9 — Proactive refresh secret drift Post-refresh reconstruction yields wrong secret Zero-polynomial shares summed incorrectly; epoch mismatch Reconstruct test with known value after refresh None Rollback to pre-refresh shares; re-run refresh Halt signing; audit refresh transcript Epoch counter; refresh transcripts Refresh correctness test (I8) run after every epoch
F10 — CSPRNG failure / entropy starvation Low-entropy shares; distinguishable polynomials Platform CSPRNG depletion; VM fork without reseed OS error on entropy source; runtime check for getrandom errors None (abort on entropy error) Ensure entropy source healthy; retry Block share generation until entropy restored OS error log Use getrandom crate; fail-closed if entropy unavailable
F11 — CLI share file decryption failure Err(MacVerificationFailed) on share load Wrong passphrase; file corruption; tampered file MAC check on load None Restore from backup Audit file access log; check for tampering MAC error; file hash Encrypted + MACed share files; backup policy
F12 — Lagrange coefficient overflow / zero denominator Panic or Err(SingularInterpolation) Duplicate ids reaching Lagrange (should be caught earlier); zero field element in denominator Field inverse returns Err on zero input None (arithmetic error) Ensure distinct non-zero ids Audit share generation; re-issue Error log with ids I10 input validation; field inv returns Err not panic
F13 — Round message timeout in tss-protocol Round stalls; parties waiting indefinitely Network partition; crashed party; Byzantine drop Per-round deadline; DkgRound / FROST round watchdog Timeout → identifiable abort; log missing responders Retry with live parties if ≥ t respond Investigate non-responding party Timeout log; absent party ids Configurable round timeouts; async channel with deadline
F14 — Dependency regression (dalek, blst) Test failures; verification regressions Upstream breaking change or security patch CI runs conformance + RFC 9591 test vectors on every dep update CI gate blocks merge Downgrade dep; audit changelog Review audit report; update or pin CI failure log; diff of dep Pinned versions in Cargo.lock; cargo audit gating

Operational Readiness

Metrics Strategy

Metric Type Labels Alert Threshold
shamir.split.duration_ms histogram t, n p99 > 50 ms
shamir.reconstruct.duration_ms histogram t, n p99 > 50 ms
vss.commit_verify.fail_total counter dealer_id > 0 triggers complaint
dkg.round_duration_ms histogram round (Commit/Share/Complaint/Finalize) p99 > configured timeout
dkg.disqualified_total counter — > 0 per session
frost.partial_fail_total counter signer_id > 0 triggers abort
frost.sign_round1_duration_ms histogram — p99 > 200 ms
frost.sign_round2_duration_ms histogram — p99 > 200 ms
bls.partial_fail_total counter signer_id > 0 triggers abort
bls.aggregate_duration_ms histogram — p99 > 500 ms
proactive.refresh_duration_ms histogram — p99 > 5 s
proto.replay_detected_total counter session_id > 0 alerts
field.out_of_range_total counter — > 0 alerts
csprng.error_total counter — > 0 pages on-call
cli.share_load_fail_total counter — > 0 alerts

Tracing Strategy

  • Span per protocol session: dkg_session{session_id}, frost_session{session_id}, bls_session{session_id}, proactive_refresh{epoch}.
  • Child spans per round: dkg_round{round_name}, frost_round1, frost_round2.
  • Attributes on spans: t, n, signer_count, qualified_count, complaint_count.
  • CRITICAL: spans must NOT include Scalar / Share values — only ids, counts, booleans (pass/fail).
  • Trace backend: OpenTelemetry SDK via opentelemetry crate; exporters pluggable (OTLP, Jaeger).
  • Session-id propagated as W3C trace-context header in CLI simulation mode.

Structured Logging

Event Level Fields Notes
Share generation INFO t, n, session_id No share values
Commitment publish INFO dealer_id, commit_hash Hash of commitment vector
Complaint raised WARN from, against, round Include commitment mismatch flag
Party disqualified WARN party_id, reason Reason enum: BadShare / Timeout / FalseComplaint
DKG finalized INFO session_id, qualified_n, joint_pubkey_hex
FROST round 1 done INFO session_id, commitment_count
FROST partial fail ERROR session_id, signer_id Triggers identifiable-abort path
Signature produced INFO session_id, R_hex, signers No private data
Nonce reuse detected ERROR session_id Immediate abort
Proactive refresh done INFO epoch, party_count, duration_ms
CSPRNG error ERROR os_error Pages on-call

All fields are key=value; no secret material in any log field.

Dashboard Design

ROW 1 — Health: csprng_errors | replay_detected | frost_partial_fails | bls_partial_fails
ROW 2 — DKG: dkg sessions started/finalized | disqualified_total | round durations heatmap
ROW 3 — Signing (FROST): round1/round2 duration p50/p95/p99 | sign sessions/hr
ROW 4 — Signing (BLS): aggregate duration p50/p99 | partial_fail rate
ROW 5 — Share ops: split/reconstruct duration | vss_fail_total | cli_share_load_fail
ROW 6 — Proactive: refresh duration | epoch lag | recovery events

Alerting Philosophy

  • Page immediately: csprng.error_total > 0, proto.replay_detected_total > 0, frost.nonce_reuse > 0.
  • Ticket (non-paging): dkg.disqualified_total > 0, frost.partial_fail_total > 0, bls.partial_fail_total > 0, vss.commit_verify.fail_total > 0.
  • SLO breach: p99 latencies exceed thresholds for two consecutive windows.
  • Alert fatigue rule: vss.commit_verify.fail_total grouped by dealer_id; noisy single dealer does not mask systemic issues.

SLO Definitions

SLO Target Measurement Window
Shamir split correctness 100% of splits produce t-reconstructible shares Per batch in CI
FROST sign success (given t honest signers) 99.9% sessions complete Rolling 30 days
DKG finalization success (given honest majority) 99.5% sessions finalize Rolling 30 days
Proactive refresh non-disruption Secret unchanged in 100% of refresh epochs Per epoch, CI check
CLI share load (MAC valid) 100% Per operation
RFC 9591 FROST test vector pass 100% Every CI run

Incident Response

  1. Detect — alert fires or operator notices anomaly on dashboard.
  2. Triage — check csprng.error, replay_detected, frost.nonce_reuse first (critical path).
  3. Contain — halt signing sessions if nonce reuse or replay confirmed; halt DKG if CSPRNG failure.
  4. Diagnose — pull structured logs for session_id; inspect DKG transcript; check epoch counter.
  5. Remediate — follow relevant runbook (below).
  6. Post-mortem — root-cause analysis; update threat table if new attack surface found.

Runbooks

Runbook Trigger Key Steps
RB-01: Nonce Reuse frost.nonceReuse alert Abort all FROST sessions; rotate key shares via proactive refresh; audit nonce storage code
RB-02: DKG Stall DKG round timeout Identify non-responding parties via absent-party log; restart DKG with qualified set ≥ t
RB-03: Share Corruption vss.commit_verify.fail_total > 0 Identify bad dealer; raise complaint; proceed with qualified set; trigger share recovery for affected party
RB-04: CSPRNG Failure csprng.error_total > 0 Block all secret generation; restart OS entropy pool; verify getrandom returns; re-enable after entropy confirmed
RB-05: Supply-chain Alert cargo audit reports advisory Freeze deployments; assess severity; downgrade or patch dep; re-run conformance suite
RB-06: Proactive Refresh Drift Reconstruct yields wrong secret post-refresh Halt signing; rollback to pre-refresh shares (backup); audit refresh transcript; fix epoch logic; re-run refresh
RB-07: CLI Share File Tamper cli.share_load_fail_total > 0 Restore from backup; audit file access logs; revoke and re-issue shares
RB-08: Dependency Regression CI conformance failures after dep bump Bisect dep version; pin to last-known-good; open upstream issue; re-enable CI

Backup and Restore Strategy

  • Share files encrypted (ChaCha20-Poly1305) and MACed at rest; version header [tss-share v1].
  • Backup frequency: after every proactive refresh epoch + on initial share creation.
  • Restore path: load encrypted backup → verify MAC → check epoch counter matches current epoch → use in reconstruction.
  • Cold backup (offline, air-gapped) recommended for n threshold configurations where n is small.
  • Key material for share encryption derived from operator passphrase via Argon2id; never stored alongside share file.

Deployment Topology

Typical threshold deployment (t-of-n parties):
  Party 1..n: each runs tss-cli / embeds tss-protocol
  Coordinator (optional): runs tss-protocol orchestrator for round message relay
  No trusted dealer in DKG mode; coordinator is untrusted relay only
  CLI simulation: all n parties in one process with in-memory message bus
  Production: parties communicate over mTLS channels; coordinator provides ordering only

Canary, Rolling Upgrade, Rollback, Migration

Concern Strategy
Canary Deploy new tss-cli version to 1-of-n party first; run DKG + sign health check before rolling to all
Rolling upgrade Upgrade one party at a time; protocol must be backward-compatible within the current wire version
Rollback Pin Cargo.lock; git checkout to prior tag; re-run conformance suite before rejoining
Migration (share format) Introduce v2 share header; deserializer accepts both v1 and v2 during migration window; v1 write deprecated after all parties upgraded
Key rotation Proactive refresh (Phase 12) changes share encoding without changing secret; compatible with rolling upgrade

Compatibility & Versioning

Wire and Message Compatibility

Format Version Field Backward-Compatible Changes Breaking Changes
DKG round messages u8 version in header Add optional fields with default; extend enum variants Change scalar encoding; remove required fields
FROST round messages u8 version Add new commitment types; extend BindingFactor hash alg enum Change NonceCommitment layout; alter challenge hash
BLS partial signature u8 version Add curve-id field for multi-curve support Change point serialization; alter aggregation order
Share files (CLI) [tss-share vN] text header Add optional metadata fields Change encryption scheme; alter MAC tag position
Proactive refresh messages u8 version + u32 epoch Add auxiliary data fields Change epoch encoding; alter zero-polynomial format

All wire types use canonical serialization (little-endian scalars, compressed EC points per SEC1); documented in tss-core.

Storage and Format Compatibility

  • Share serialization: ShareId(u32) + Scalar (32-byte LE); stable across patch versions.
  • Commitment (Vec<Point>): length-prefixed array of compressed points; version-tagged.
  • KeyShare file: CBOR or bincode with explicit field tags; new optional fields must have defaults.
  • Format version embedded in every persisted artifact; loader rejects unknown major versions.

Type and Schema Evolution (Core Types)

Type Allowed Evolution Prohibited
Scalar None (fixed 32-byte LE field element) Change field prime; change byte order
ShareId(u32) None (wire-stable u32) Widen to u64 without wire-version bump
ThresholdParams Add optional fields (e.g., max_n) with defaults Change semantics of existing t, n
NonceCommitment Add new hash algorithm variants under enum Remove existing variant
DkgRound enum Add new variant at end Reorder or remove variants
AggregateSignature None without wire-version bump Change layout of (R, z) pair

API Stability

  • tss-core, tss-field, tss-shamir, tss-group public APIs: semver-stable after 1.0; no breaking changes without major version bump.
  • tss-protocol message types: minor-version additive-only changes; breaking changes require major bump and migration guide.
  • tss-cli subcommand interface: flags stable after 1.0; new subcommands allowed in minor; flag removal requires major.
  • Internal crates (tss-dkg, tss-schnorr, tss-bls, tss-proactive): semver-stable public API; implementation details (struct field visibility) unstable until 1.0.

Migration Guarantees

Migration Guarantee Procedure
Share format v1 → v2 No secret loss; both versions reconstruct the same secret Deploy v2 reader accepting v1; write new shares as v2; deprecate v1 after full rollout
t/n parameter change (reshare) Secret preserved (I8); new t/n encoded in new shares Run additive reshare (Phase 4); distribute new shares; confirm reconstruction; retire old shares
Curve migration (e.g., ed25519 → ristretto) Not in-place; requires new DKG Run DKG on new curve; sign a migration attestation; retire old key shares
Proactive refresh epoch rollover Secret unchanged (I8); epoch counter increments monotonically Complete refresh across all parties before epoch deadline; operator verifies epoch counter parity

Deprecation Policy

  • Features deprecated in minor version N; removed no earlier than minor version N+2 (or next major).
  • Deprecated items annotated #[deprecated(since = "x.y", note = "...")] in Rust source.
  • Wire format versions: old version support maintained for two minor releases after new version ships.
  • Removal announced in CHANGELOG with migration path and timeline.

Semantic Versioning Policy

Version Component Meaning
Major Breaking wire format, breaking public API, breaking share format
Minor New protocol feature, new crate, new CLI subcommand, additive type evolution
Patch Bug fix, performance, documentation, non-breaking dependency update
  • Cargo.lock pinned in all binaries; library crates specify ^x.y bounds.
  • cargo audit run on every CI push; critical advisories block release.

Rolling-Upgrade Guarantees

  • Within a minor version: a party running version N and a party running version N+1 can interoperate in DKG and signing sessions provided the wire-version field is negotiated.
  • Wire-version negotiation: lower of the two versions' supported range is used; newer features disabled if peer does not support.
  • No rolling upgrades across major versions without explicit migration step; operator must drain and restart all sessions.
  • Conformance suite (RFC 9591 test vectors + internal round-trip tests) must pass before any release is tagged.

Testing & Verification Strategy

Unit Tests — per-crate, per-type

Crate What How
tss-field Field axioms (closure, assoc, comm, distrib, identity, inverse) Property: proptest over random Scalars; compare with reference BigUint
tss-field Inverse correctness: a * inv(a) == 1 for non-zero Property + exhaustive for small modulus fixture
tss-field Reject inverse of zero Unit: assert!(inv(0).is_err())
tss-shamir Split reconstructs to same secret for all (t,n) in {1..=8}^2 where t<=n Table-driven unit test; check reconstruct(split(s,t,n)[0..t]) == s
tss-shamir Degree invariant: polynomial has exactly t-1 non-leading zeros Unit: inspect poly.coeffs.len() == t
tss-shamir Under-threshold: any t-1 shares reconstruct to statistically independent value Statistical: sample 1000 reconstructions from random t-1 subsets; Chi-square uniform
tss-shamir Duplicate ShareId rejected Unit: inject duplicate; expect Err(DuplicateShareId)
tss-vss Feldman: valid share passes verification Unit per (t,n) fixture
tss-vss Feldman: tampered share fails Unit: flip one bit in share.value; expect Err(VerificationFailed)
tss-vss Pedersen: commitment is hiding — reveals nothing about secret Property: two different secrets produce commitments with identical distribution
tss-dkg Round-state transitions: Commit→Share→Complaint→Finalize State machine unit: drive all transitions with mocked messages
tss-dkg Honest-party joint pubkey correctness Fixture with n=5 parties, compare sum(g^a_i_0) against DKG output
tss-schnorr Partial signature accepted by partial-verification equation Unit per signing set
tss-schnorr Aggregate fails if any z_i tampered Unit: inject bad z_i
tss-bls Pairing equation holds for valid aggregate Unit: fixture over stub pairing
tss-proactive Refresh produces new shares summing to same secret Unit: reconstruct before and after refresh
tss-core ThresholdParams rejects t=0, t>n Unit: all invalid combinations up to n=20
tss-core Zeroize: share memory zeroed after drop Unsafe memory inspection fixture

Property-Based Tests (proptest / quickcheck)

  • ∀ (t,n,s): reconstruct(split(s,t,n)) == s over random Scalar s and valid (t,n).
  • ∀ (t,n): any t-1 subset of n shares fails to recover s — verify uniform randomness via distribution test.
  • Field axioms: commutativity, associativity, distributivity over 10k random pairs.
  • Lagrange coefficient sum: sum(lambda_i for i in S) == 1 over random t-subsets (weighted identity check).
  • Reshare invariance: ∀ epoch transitions: reconstruct(new_shares) == reconstruct(old_shares).
  • FROST binding factor: distinct signers or messages → distinct rho_i values (collision resistance proxy).
  • VSS soundness: random polynomial + commitment → each honest share verifies; perturbed share fails.

Integration Tests — multi-crate flows

Scenario Parties Assertion
Full SSS round-trip Dealer + n=7 holders reconstruct from any t=4 subset == original secret
Feldman VSS end-to-end Dealer + 5 verifiers all verify; dealer changes one coeff -> fail
Pedersen VSS end-to-end Dealer + 5 verifiers all verify; blinding values distinct each run
DKG no-fault n=5, t=3 parties joint pubkey == product of constant-term commitments
DKG with one bad dealer n=5, t=3 bad dealer disqualified; joint key from qualified set
FROST signing no-fault n=5, t=3, chosen signers (R,z) verifies under joint pubkey
FROST identifiable abort n=5, t=3, one party sends bad z_i bad party identified by name; remaining sign successfully
Proactive refresh n=5, t=3, 3 epochs reconstruct stable across all epochs
BLS threshold n=5, t=3 aggregate pairing verify passes
CLI split + reconstruct CLI subprocess stdout share lines round-trip to secret

Differential Tests — conformance against references

Component Reference Method
FROST partial sign + aggregate RFC 9591 Appendix B test vectors Parse vectors from JSON; drive tss-schnorr with same nonces/keys; compare (R,z) byte-for-byte
Shamir split/reconstruct sharks crate, vsss-rs crate Same (t,n,secret) triple → verify reconstruct matches; split output may differ (randomness)
Feldman VSS verify frost-core reference Feed same commitment + share; verify pass/fail matches
BLS partial sign Drand reference protocol (go-drand) Load go-drand test fixture keys/shares; drive tss-bls; check aggregate byte equality
Field arithmetic num-bigint BigUint over same prime Cross-check add/mul/inv results for 10k random pairs

Fuzzing (cargo-fuzz + libFuzzer)

Fuzz target Input grammar Goal
fuzz_reconstruct Random byte slice → Vec<Share> panic-free; no UB; Err not panic on malformed
fuzz_vss_verify (commitment bytes, share bytes) panic-free; no secret leak in Err messages
fuzz_dkg_message Arbitrary DkgRound messages state machine never panics; bad messages -> Err
fuzz_frost_partial (partial_sig bytes, nonce_commit bytes) panic-free; aggregation of random partials is Err
fuzz_cli_args argv strings CLI never panics; exit codes only
fuzz_polynomial_eval (coeffs bytes, point bytes) no UB; no integer overflow

All fuzz targets run for 10 M iterations in CI; corpus seeds from RFC vectors and hand-crafted edge cases.

Chaos and Fault Injection

  • DKG complaint round: inject bad VSS shares from 1, then 2, then t-1 parties; verify qualified set contracts correctly and joint key computed from remaining.
  • FROST abort: inject zero partial sig, out-of-range z_i, wrong binding factor; verify identifiable-abort names the right party.
  • Proactive refresh: simulate one party dropping out mid-refresh; verify remaining parties can complete reshare.
